**text**　位置圖必須是一個多邊形特征類。 輸出將基于來自 LRS 網絡、 日志層、 位置層和引用層的選定特性的交叉點來創建。 引用層必須是 LRS 點事件特征類或注冊到指定 LRS 網絡的 LRS 交叉特征類。 在與位置層不重疊的路徑中, 未分類值將包含在輸出位置欄位中。 您可以從相同的特性類中添加多個日志和位置域, 方法是創建選擇層, 并將每個選擇層指定為日志欄位或位置欄位。 例如, 如果您有 LRS 線事件特征類, 您可以創建兩個選擇層- A 和 B 類- 和 B 。指定每個選擇層為日志欄位。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The location layer must be a polygon feature class.\nThe output will be created based on the intersection of selected features from the LRS Network, log layer(s), location layer(s), and referent layer.\nThe referent layer must be an LRS point event feature class or an LRS intersection feature class that is registered to the specified LRS Network.\nA value of unclassified will be included in the output location fields for routes that do not overlap with the location layers.\nYou can add multiple log and location fields from the same feature class by creating selection layers and specifying each selection layer as either a log field or a location field. For example, if you have an LRS line event feature class, you can create two selection layers—Class A and Class B—and specify each selection layer as a log field.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html#usage

## 生成線性參考路徑日志( Learar 引用工具) · 參數

**id**　d14b85a777c9427dee-10

**english**　Generate Linear Referenced Route Log

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 資料產品工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html

**parameter**　referent_location

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 參考地點(可選)
- 指定用于計算引用偏移的方法。 None - 將不計算引用偏移。 這是默認值。 最近的上流 - 路線日志條目中最接近的引用特征將用來計算引用偏移。 如果沒有上游引用特征, 參考值將是空的。 最接近的 - 路徑日志條目中最接近的引用特征, 上游或下游, 將用來計算引用偏移 。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Referent Location (Optional)", "Specifies the method that will be used to calculate the referent offset. None—The referent offset will not be calculated. This is the default. Nearest Upstream—The nearest referent feature upstream of the route log entry will be used to calculate the referent offset. If no upstream referent feature exists, the referent values will be empty. Nearest—The nearest referent feature, upstream or downstream of the route log entry, will be used to calculate the referent offset.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html#parameters

## 生成線性參考路徑日志( Learar 引用工具) · 參數

**id**　d14b85a777c9427dee-13

**english**　Generate Linear Referenced Route Log

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 資料產品工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html

**parameter**　offset_units

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 偏移單位( 可選)
- 指定用于計算參考特征和路線日志輸入之間的距離的偏移單位。英寸(美國調查)——單位為英寸。腳(美國調查)——單位為英尺。Yard(美國調查)——單位為碼。Miles(美國調查)——單位為英里。遠(美國調查)——單位為海里。美航邁爾(美國調查)——單位為海里。腳(國際)——單位為國際英尺。這是默認的。《規約》邁爾斯——單位為法定英里。毫米——單位為毫米。厘米——單位為厘米。內徑——單位為厘米。米——單位為米。米——單位為米。千米——單位為米。公里——單位為米。將到千米。12步,單位將到千米。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Offset Units (Optional)", "Specifies the offset units that will be used to calculate the distance between the referent feature and the route log entry. Inches (US Survey)—The units will be inches. Feet (US Survey)—The units will be feet. Yards (US Survey)—The units will be yards. Miles (US Survey)—The units will be miles. Nautical Miles (US Survey)—The units will be nautical miles. Feet (International)—The units will be international feet. This is the default. Statute Miles—The units will be statute miles. Millimeters—The units will be millimeters. Centimeters—The units will be centimeters. Meters—The units will be meters. Kilometers—The units will be kilometers. Decimeters—The units will be decimeters.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html#parameters

- 指定用于計算參考特征和路線日志輸入之間的距離的抵消單位。INCHES - 單位為英寸。FEET - 單位為英尺。YARDS - 單位為英尺。YARDS - 單位為碼。MILES - 單位為英里。 NAUTIC_ MILES - 單位將為海里英里。 INTFEET - 單位為國際英尺。這是默認值。INTMILES - 單位為法定英里。 MILMETERS - 單位為毫米。 CENTIMETEMETERS - 單位為厘米。METERS - 單位為米。METERS - 單位為米。KIORMETES - 單位為公里。DECIMETENS。- 單位將會是十進制。

INCHES—The units will be inches. FEET—The units will be feet. YARDS—The units will be yards. MILES—The units will be miles. NAUTICAL_MILES—The units will be nautical miles. INTFEET—The units will be international feet. This is the default. INTMILES—The units will be statute miles. MILLIMETERS—The units will be millimeters. CENTIMETERS—The units will be centimeters. METERS—The units will be meters. KILOMETERS—The units will be kilometers. DECIMETERS—The units will be decimeters.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

## 生成線性參考路徑日志( Learar 引用工具) · 代碼樣本

**id**　d14b85a777c9427dee-31

**english**　Generate Linear Referenced Route Log

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 資料產品工具

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html

**text**　# Name: GenerateLRRouteLog_ex1.py
# Description: Create a route log data product that returns the location and information of signal events,
# functional class events, reference posts, intersections, and county boundaries for routes in an LRS Network
# Requirements: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Set tool variables
in_route_features = "CountyLog"
effective_date = "12/31/2024"
log_fields = "Signal SIGNAL_MODE; Functional_Class FUNCTIONAL_CLASS; Intersections IntersectionName"
merge_coincident_events = "DO_NOT_MERGE"
location_fields = "Counties NAME"
referent_location = "NEAREST_UPSTREAM"
referent_features = "Reference_Post"
referent_field = "POST_NAME"
offset_units = "INTFEET"
output_format = "TABLE"
out_file = None
out_table = r"C:\Data\SampleData.gdb\RL1"

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.GenerateLRRouteLog(in_route_features, effective_date, log_fields, merge_coincident_events,
                                location_fields, referent_location, referent_features, referent_field,
                                offset_units, output_format, out_file, out_table)

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "# Name: GenerateLRRouteLog_ex1.py\n# Description: Create a route log data product that returns the location and information of signal events,\n# functional class events, reference posts, intersections, and county boundaries for routes in an LRS Network\n# Requirements: ArcGIS Linear Referencing\n\n# Set tool variables\nin_route_features = \"CountyLog\"\neffective_date = \"12/31/2024\"\nlog_fields = \"Signal SIGNAL_MODE; Functional_Class FUNCTIONAL_CLASS; Intersections IntersectionName\"\nmerge_coincident_events = \"DO_NOT_MERGE\"\nlocation_fields = \"Counties NAME\"\nreferent_location = \"NEAREST_UPSTREAM\"\nreferent_features = \"Reference_Post\"\nreferent_field = \"POST_NAME\"\noffset_units = \"INTFEET\"\noutput_format = \"TABLE\"\nout_file = None\nout_table = r\"C:\\Data\\SampleData.gdb\\RL1\"\n\n# Run the tool\narcpy.lr.GenerateLRRouteLog(in_route_features, effective_date, log_fields, merge_coincident_events,\n                                location_fields, referent_location, referent_features, referent_field,\n                                offset_units, output_format, out_file, out_table)", "section": "Code sample"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html#parameters

**text**　# Name: GenerateLRRouteLog_ex2.py
# Description: For routes in an LRS Network, create a route log data product that returns the location and information of anomalies, pipes, crossing utility intersections, operating pressure,
# ILI surveys, as well as county and city boundaries
# Requirements: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Import arcpy module
import arcpy

# Set current workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:\Data\SampleData.gdb"

# Using the Anomaly point event feature class, create a feature layer that contains active anomalies, to be used as a log layer
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer("Anomaly", "Anomaly_Active", "anomalystatus = 'Active'")

# Using the Pipes line event feature class, create three feature layers, each representing a different pipe material, to be used as log layers
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer("Pipes", "Pipes_CastIron", "MATERIAL = 2")
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer("Pipes", "Pipes_Copper", "MATERIAL = 4")
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer("Pipes", "Pipes_Composite", "MATERIAL = 10")

# Set tool variables
in_route_features = "Engineering Network"
effective_date = "12/31/2024"
log_fields = "Anomaly_Active anomalytype; Crossing Utility Intersection intersectionname; Pipes_CastIron status; Pipes_Copper status; Pipes_Composite status; Operating Pressure pressurevalue"
merge_coincident_events = "DO_NOT_MERGE"
location_fields = "Counties NAME; Cities CITY_NM"
referent_location = "NEAREST_UPSTREAM"
referent_features = "ILI Survey Readings"
referent_field = "surveytype"
offset_units = "INTFEET"
output_format = "TABLE"
out_file = None
out_table = r"C:\Data\SampleData.gdb\RL2"

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.GenerateLRRouteLog(in_route_features, effective_date, log_fields, merge_coincident_events,
                                location_fields, referent_location, referent_features, referent_field,
                                offset_units, output_format, out_file, out_table)

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

Crossing Utility Intersection intersectionname; Pipes_CastIron status; Pipes_Copper status; Pipes_Composite status; Operating Pressure pressurevalue\"\nmerge_coincident_events = \"DO_NOT_MERGE\"\nlocation_fields = \"Counties NAME; Cities CITY_NM\"\nreferent_location = \"NEAREST_UPSTREAM\"\nreferent_features = \"ILI Survey Readings\"\nreferent_field = \"surveytype\"\noffset_units = \"INTFEET\"\noutput_format = \"TABLE\"\nout_file = None\nout_table = r\"C:\\Data\\SampleData.gdb\\RL2\"\n\n# Run the tool\narcpy.lr.GenerateLRRouteLog(in_route_features, effective_date, log_fields, merge_coincident_events,\n                                location_fields, referent_location, referent_features, referent_field,\n                                offset_units, output_format, out_file, out_table)", "section": "Code sample"}

**kind**　程式或語法

**text**　# Name: GenerateLRRouteLog_ex3.py
# Description: Create a route log data product that returns the location and information of crashes, speed limits, pavement conditions,
# centerlines, intersections, and city boundaries for routes in a combined LRS and Address Data Management dataset that is published as a feature service
# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Import arcpy module
import arcpy

# Data is in a feature service. Signing in to the Enterprise portal is required to access the feature service
arcpy.SignInToPortal('https://yourdomain.com/portal', 'username', 'password')

# Map the LRS Network from the feature service. Here, 11 corresponds to the LRS Network's layer ID
in_route_features = r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/11"

# Create the feature layers from the feature service. The feature layers will be used as log layers
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/25", "crash")
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/24", "intersections")
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/17", "speed_limit")
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/14", "pavement_condition")
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/19", "centerline")

# Create a feature layer from the Cities layer of the feature service to be used as the location layer
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/21", "cities")

# Map the Reference Post layer from the feature service to be used as the input to the referent_features parameter
referent_features = r"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/26"

# Set tool variables
effective_date = "12/31/2024"
log_fields = "crash CrashType; intersections IntersectionName; speed_limit SpeedLimit; pavement_condition Condition; centerline fullname"
merge_coincident_events = "MERGE"
location_fields = "cities name"
referent_location = "NEAREST"
referent_field = "PostName"
offset_units = "INTFEET"
output_format = "CSV"
out_file = r"C:\Data\RL3.csv"
out_table = None

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.GenerateLRRouteLog(in_route_features, effective_date, log_fields, merge_coincident_events,
                                location_fields, referent_location, referent_features, referent_field,
                                offset_units, output_format, out_file, out_table)

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

Signing in to the Enterprise portal is required to access the feature service\narcpy.SignInToPortal('https://yourdomain.com/portal', 'username', 'password')\n\n# Map the LRS Network from the feature service. Here, 11 corresponds to the LRS Network's layer ID\nin_route_features = r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/11\"\n\n# Create the feature layers from the feature service. The feature layers will be used as log layers\narcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/25\", \"crash\")\narcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/24\", \"intersections\")\narcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/17\", \"speed_limit\")\narcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/14\", \"pavement_condition\")\narcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/19\", \"centerline\")\n\n# Create a feature layer from the Cities layer of the feature service to be used as the location layer\narcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/21\", \"cities\")\n\n# Map the Reference Post layer from the feature service to be used as the input to the referent_features parameter\nreferent_features = r\"https://yourdomain.com/server/rest/services/FeatureServiceName/FeatureServer/26\"\n\n# Set tool variables\neffective_date = \"12/31/2024\"\nlog_fields = \"crash CrashType; intersections IntersectionName; speed_limit SpeedLimit; pavement_condition Condition; centerline fullname\"\nmerge_coincident_events = \"MERGE\"\nlocation_fields = \"cities name\"\nreferent_location = \"NEAREST\"\nreferent_field = \"PostName\"\noffset_units = \"INTFEET\"\noutput_format = \"CSV\"\nout_file = r\"C:\\Data\\RL3.csv\"\nout_table = None\n\n# Run the tool\narcpy.lr.GenerateLRRouteLog(in_route_features, effective_date, log_fields, merge_coincident_events,\n                                location_fields, referent_location, referent_features, referent_field,\n                                offset_units, output_format, out_file, out_table)", "section": "Code sample"}

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_route_features", "label_zh": "輸入線路特征", "label_en": "Input Route Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The route features that will be used to generate the route log."}, {"parameter": "effective_date", "label_zh": "生效日期", "label_en": "Effective Date", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Date", "description_en": "The date that will be used to define the temporal view of the network. The default value is today's date."}, {"parameter": "log_fields", "label_zh": "日志欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "Log Fields (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The fields that will be used to show event and intersection information in the output. Value table columns: Layer—The feature layer that will be used as the log layer. Field—The field that will participate in the route log."}, {"parameter": "merge_coincident_events", "label_zh": "合并巧合事件( 可選擇)", "label_en": "Merge coincident events (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether coincident line events will be merged in the output. Checked—Coincident line events with the same value in the log field will be merged and returned as a single event. Unchecked—Coincident line events with the same value in the log field will not be merged or returned as a single event. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "location_fields", "label_zh": "欄位(可選)", "label_en": "Location Fields (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The fields used to show the attributes of the polygon boundaries crossed by the routes. Value table columns: Layer—The feature layer that will be used as the location layer. Field—The field that contains the attributes of the polygon boundaries crossed by the routes."}, {"parameter": "referent_location", "label_zh": "參考地點(可選)", "label_en": "Referent Location (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the method that will be used to calculate the referent offset. None—The referent offset will not be calculated. This is the default. Nearest Upstream—The nearest referent feature upstream of the route log entry will be used to calculate the referent offset. If no upstream referent feature exists, the referent values will be empty. Nearest—The nearest referent feature, upstream or downstream of the route log entry, will be used to calculate the referent offset."}, {"parameter": "referent_features", "label_zh": "公文特征(可選)", "label_en": "Referent Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The feature layer that will be used to calculate the offset from the route log entry."}, {"parameter": "referent_field", "label_zh": "參考欄位(可選)", "label_en": "Referent Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The referent field that will contain the attributes of the referent features in the output."}, {"parameter": "offset_units", "label_zh": "偏移單位( 可選)", "label_en": "Offset Units (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the offset units that will be used to calculate the distance between the referent feature and the route log entry. Inches (US Survey)—The units will be inches. Feet (US Survey)—The units will be feet. Yards (US Survey)—The units will be yards. Miles (US Survey)—The units will be miles. Nautical Miles (US Survey)—The units will be nautical miles. Feet (International)—The units will be international feet. This is the default. Statute Miles—The units will be statute miles. Millimeters—The units will be millimeters. Centimeters—The units will be centimeters. Meters—The units will be meters. Kilometers—The units will be kilometers. Decimeters—The units will be decimeters."}, {"parameter": "output_format", "label_zh": "輸出格式( 可選)", "label_en": "Output Format (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the format of the output. CSV—The output will be a .csv file. This is the default. Table—The output will be a geodatabase table."}, {"parameter": "out_file", "label_zh": "輸出文件( 可選)", "label_en": "Output File (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "The output .csv file that will contain the route log."}, {"parameter": "out_table", "label_zh": "產出表(備選)", "label_en": "Output Table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "The output geodatabase table that will contain the route log."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.lr.GenerateLRRouteLog(in_route_features, effective_date, {log_fields}, {merge_coincident_events}, {location_fields}, {referent_location}, {referent_features}, {referent_field}, {offset_units}, {output_format}, {out_file}, {out_table})

## 生成線性參考路徑日志( Learar 引用工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 資料產品工具

**english**　Generate Linear Referenced Route Log

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html

**id**　d14b85a777c9427dee-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generate Linear Referenced Route Log，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入線路特征 [in_route_features]、生效日期 [effective_date]、日志欄位( 可選) [log_fields]、合并巧合事件( 可選擇) [merge_coincident_events]、欄位(可選) [location_fields]、參考地點(可選) [referent_location]、公文特征(可選) [referent_features]、參考欄位(可選) [referent_field]、偏移單位( 可選) [offset_units]、輸出格式( 可選) [output_format]、輸出文件( 可選) [out_file]、產出表(備選) [out_table]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/generate-linear-referenced-route-log.html#parameters
